(function () {
  var serverDecision = "gate";
  var decisionKey = "__OPGG_RESIDUAL_GOOGLE_DECISION__";
  var decisionEvent = "opgg-residual-google-decision";

  function setMetadata(decision) {
    var width = window.screen && window.screen.width;
    var height = window.screen && window.screen.height;
    var screenSize = Number.isInteger(width) && Number.isInteger(height) && width >= 0 && width <= 10000 && height >= 0 && height <= 10000 ? width + 'x' + height : 'unknown';
    window.gtag('set', 'user_properties', {
      opgg_gate_decision: decision,
      opgg_gate_screen: screenSize,
      opgg_gate_release: "production-482-1-c379cdcc",
    });
  }

  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  window.gtag = window.gtag || function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
  if (serverDecision !== 'gate') {
    setMetadata(serverDecision);
    return;
  }

  setMetadata('pending');
  function onDecision() {
    var decision = window[decisionKey];
    if (decision === 'gate_loaded' || decision === 'gate_suppressed' || decision === 'gate_failed_open') {
      setMetadata(decision);
      window.removeEventListener(decisionEvent, onDecision);
    }
  }
  window.addEventListener(decisionEvent, onDecision);
  onDecision();
})();

(function () {
  var residualDecisionKey = "__OPGG_RESIDUAL_GOOGLE_DECISION__";
  var residualDecisionEvent = "opgg-residual-google-decision";
  var suppressionFlagKey = "__OPGG_SUPPRESS_GA_ADS__";
  var clientRulesKey = "__OPGG_AD_SUPPRESS_CLIENT_RULES__";
  var matchedRuleKey = "__OPGG_AD_SUPPRESS_MATCHED_RULE__";
  var cookieName = "_sp";
  var cookieDomain = ".op.gg";
  var cookieValue = "1";
  var gaId = "G-HKZFKE5JEL";
  var gtmId = "GTM-WKTM6W8T";
  var supportedConditionKeys = ["absentCookieNames","devicePixelRatio","nonUserAgentPattern","pathPrefixes","referrer","requiredLanguagePrefixes","screen","userAgentPattern","viewport"];
  var authCookieNames = ["_ot","gpm-token"];
  var loadedKey = '__OPGG_GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_GATE_LOADED__';

  function loadSuppressedReviewAnalytics(reason) {
    var loadedKey = '__OPGG_SUPPRESSED_REVIEW_GA_LOADED__';
    if (window[loadedKey]) {
      return;
    }
    window[loadedKey] = true;
    window.opggReviewLolDataLayer = window.opggReviewLolDataLayer || [];
    function reviewGtag() { window.opggReviewLolDataLayer.push(arguments); }
    reviewGtag('js', new Date());
    reviewGtag('config', "G-YBSKRT4KZV", {
      suppression_reason: reason,
      cookie_prefix: 'opgg_review_lol',
      allow_google_signals: false,
      allow_ad_personalization_signals: false,
    });
    var script = document.createElement('script');
    script.id = 'opgg-suppressed-review-ga';
    script.src = 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=' +
      encodeURIComponent("G-YBSKRT4KZV") +
      '&l=opggReviewLolDataLayer';
    script.async = true;
    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
  }


  function reportDecision(decision) {
    // The document's inline metadata writer queues the final value before Google configuration.
    try {
      window[residualDecisionKey] = decision;
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event(residualDecisionEvent));
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  function ensureGoogleQueue() {
    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
    window.gtag = window.gtag || function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
  }

  function appendScript(id, src, attrs) {
    if (document.getElementById(id)) {
      return;
    }

    var script = document.createElement('script');
    script.id = id;
    script.src = src;
    script.async = true;

    if (attrs) {
      Object.keys(attrs).forEach(function (key) {
        script.setAttribute(key, attrs[key]);
      });
    }

    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
  }

  function loadGoogleAnalytics() {
    if (window[loadedKey] || window[suppressionFlagKey]) {
      return;
    }

    window[loadedKey] = true;
    ensureGoogleQueue();

    window.dataLayer.push({ 'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), event: 'gtm.js' });
    appendScript('_next-gtm', 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=' + encodeURIComponent(gtmId), {
      'data-ntpc': 'GTM',
    });

    window.gtag('js', new Date());
    window.gtag('config', gaId);
    appendScript('_next-ga', 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=' + encodeURIComponent(gaId));
  }

  function inRange(value, min, max) {
    if (typeof min === 'number' && value < min) {
      return false;
    }
    if (typeof max === 'number' && value > max) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function hasCookie(cookieString, name) {
    var prefix = name + '=';
    var isAuthCookie = authCookieNames.indexOf(name) >= 0;
    return cookieString.split(';').some(function (cookie) {
      var entry = cookie.replace(/^\s+/, '');
      return entry.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && (!isAuthCookie || entry.length > prefix.length);
    });
  }

  function matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, prefixes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(prefixes) || prefixes.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    return languages.some(function (language) {
      var normalizedLanguage = String(language || '').trim().toLowerCase();

      return prefixes.some(function (prefix) {
        return normalizedLanguage.indexOf(prefix) === 0;
      });
    });
  }

  function matchesPattern(value, pattern) {
    try {
      return new RegExp(pattern).test(value);
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function matchesPathPrefixes(pathname, pathPrefixes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(pathPrefixes)) {
      return true;
    }

    var normalizedPathname = String(pathname || '').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    normalizedPathname = normalizedPathname.replace(
      /^\/(?:en|ko|ja|pl|fr|de|es|nl|da|sv|no|ru|hu|fi|tr|ro|pt|zh-cn|zh-tw|ar|sr|it|th|vi)(?=\/|$)/i,
      '',
    ) || '/';

    return pathPrefixes.some(function (pathPrefix) {
      return normalizedPathname === pathPrefix || normalizedPathname.indexOf(pathPrefix + '/') === 0;
    });
  }

  function hasOnlySupportedConditionKeys(conditions) {
    return Object.keys(conditions).every(function (key) {
      return supportedConditionKeys.indexOf(key) >= 0;
    });
  }

  function matchesRule(rule) {
    var conditions = rule && rule.clientConditions;
    if (!conditions) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!hasOnlySupportedConditionKeys(conditions)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!matchesPathPrefixes(window.location && window.location.pathname, conditions.pathPrefixes)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (conditions.viewport) {
      if (
        !inRange(window.innerWidth, conditions.viewport.minWidth, conditions.viewport.maxWidth) ||
        !inRange(window.innerHeight, conditions.viewport.minHeight, conditions.viewport.maxHeight)
      ) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    if (conditions.screen) {
      if (
        !inRange(window.screen && window.screen.width ? window.screen.width : 0, conditions.screen.minWidth, conditions.screen.maxWidth) ||
        !inRange(window.screen && window.screen.height ? window.screen.height : 0, conditions.screen.minHeight, conditions.screen.maxHeight)
      ) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    if (conditions.devicePixelRatio && !inRange(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, conditions.devicePixelRatio.min, conditions.devicePixelRatio.max)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (conditions.referrer && conditions.referrer.absent && document.referrer) {
      return false;
    }

    var languages = [];
    if (Array.isArray(window.navigator && window.navigator.languages)) {
      languages = languages.concat(window.navigator.languages);
    }
    if (window.navigator && window.navigator.language) {
      languages.push(window.navigator.language);
    }
    if (!matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, conditions.requiredLanguagePrefixes)) {
      return false;
    }

    var userAgent = (window.navigator && window.navigator.userAgent) || '';
    if (conditions.userAgentPattern && !matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.userAgentPattern)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (conditions.nonUserAgentPattern && matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.nonUserAgentPattern)) {
      return false;
    }

    var cookieString = document.cookie || '';
    var absentCookieNames = Array.isArray(conditions.absentCookieNames) ? conditions.absentCookieNames : [];
    for (var i = 0; i < absentCookieNames.length; i += 1) {
      if (hasCookie(cookieString, absentCookieNames[i])) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  }

  function writeSuppressionCookie(maxAge) {
    var cookieParts = [cookieName + '=' + cookieValue, 'Max-Age=' + maxAge, 'Path=/', 'SameSite=Lax'];
    if (window.location.hostname === 'op.gg' || window.location.hostname.slice(-6) === '.op.gg') {
      cookieParts.push('Domain=' + cookieDomain);
    }
    if (window.location.protocol === 'https:') {
      cookieParts.push('Secure');
    }
    document.cookie = cookieParts.join('; ');
  }

  function applySuppression(rule, reportInitialDecision) {
    window[suppressionFlagKey] = true;
    window["ga-disable-G-HKZFKE5JEL"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-37HQ1LKWBE"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-WCXGQGETWH"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-HG9DB5ECL8"]=true;
    if (rule) {
      window[matchedRuleKey] = rule.id;
      // Cookie persistence must not undo the current document's suppression decision.
      try { writeSuppressionCookie(rule.rememberForSeconds); } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to persist ad analytics suppression cookie', error);
      }
    }
    // Residual metadata describes the landing decision for this document.
    if (reportInitialDecision) {
      reportDecision('gate_suppressed');
    }
    var matchedRuleId = window[matchedRuleKey];
    try { loadSuppressedReviewAnalytics(typeof matchedRuleId === 'string' ? matchedRuleId : 'client_suppressed'); } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load suppressed review analytics', error);
    }
  }

  function evaluateSuppression(reportInitialDecision) {
    if (window[suppressionFlagKey]) {
      applySuppression(undefined, reportInitialDecision);
      return true;
    }
    var rules = window[clientRulesKey];
    if (!Array.isArray(rules) || rules.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }
    for (var i = 0; i < rules.length; i += 1) {
      if (matchesRule(rules[i])) {
        applySuppression(rules[i], reportInitialDecision);
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  function evaluateNavigationSuppression() {
    if (window[suppressionFlagKey]) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      evaluateSuppression(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to evaluate ad analytics suppression after navigation', error);
    }
  }

  function installNavigationGate() {
    var navigationGateKey = '__OPGG_GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_NAVIGATION_GATE__';
    var history = window.history;
    if (window[navigationGateKey] === document || !history ||
        typeof history.pushState !== 'function' || typeof history.replaceState !== 'function' ||
        typeof window.addEventListener !== 'function') {
      return;
    }
    window[navigationGateKey] = document;
    // Google wraps these methods later; evaluate after the URL changes but before its wrapper collects.
    ['pushState', 'replaceState'].forEach(function (method) {
      var original = history[method];
      history[method] = function () {
        var result = original.apply(this, arguments);
        evaluateNavigationSuppression();
        return result;
      };
    });
    window.addEventListener('popstate', evaluateNavigationSuppression);
  }

  try { installNavigationGate(); } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to install ad analytics navigation gate', error);
  }

  try {
    if (evaluateSuppression(true)) {
      return;
    }
    reportDecision('gate_loaded');
    loadGoogleAnalytics();
  } catch (error) {
    reportDecision('gate_failed_open');
    loadGoogleAnalytics();
  }
})();
